- After the second review (23 findings, all confirmed, several of them the same from two lenses; 22 fixed, 1 left): (1) A foreign number is never read as an Israeli mobile from its end: a run of digits that starts with another country's code ("+380 50 …", "+38 (050) 123-45-67", "+995 555 …", "+33 5 …", "00380 50 …") is one foreign number, in `dropped`, its line `invalidPhone`; and Excel's dropped zero is read only from a cell's first group, so "380 50 123 4567" in a phone column is no mobile either. Before, the national part was read as +9725…, in a sheet's column and in free text, and nothing on screen said so. (2, 17) A name's header word exactly ("שם העובד", "Employee name", "Name") comes before one in other words wherever each sits, and a first name's column before a name's header in other words; "שם …" or "… name" for a branch, a department, a manager, a parent, a user, a company, a shop or a role ("שם הסניף", "שם מנהל", "שם המחלקה", "שם האב", "Username", "Branch name") is never the person's, and "שם תפקיד" is the role's column. Before, the first of them won and everyone was invited under the branch's or the manager's name. (3) A role on a line is a field of its own, parted from the name by a separator, a colon or the number ("דנה 050-… בר", "בר: דנה כהן"), or the last words of a name that keeps two words ("דנה כהן ניהול 050-…"); at a line's start only with a separator after it. So a named role called like a first name or a surname ("בר", "ברמן", "טבח") is no longer read off "בר כהן", "דנה ברמן" or "יוסי טבח, 050-…", which gave that role (one built on manager too) and half a name. A line that is a role's name and a number is still `noName`. (4, 23, and 7's WhatsApp paste) WhatsApp's time, date and sender before a message ("[10:15, 26.11.2026] אורי בן דוד: ", "26.11.2026, 10:15 - אורי בן דוד: ", iOS's "[26/11/2026, 10:15:32] …" and the US "11/26/26, 10:16 PM - …") are left out of every line that carries them, before the cells are chosen (a message's next lines carry none), and the sender's own number is never the person's. (5) The refusal's focus runs only as a refusal arrives (`arrived`, as the invite form's), never again when the screen is shown from its <Activity>. (6, 12) The counts are said only when they changed since they were last said: not after "מחיקת ההזמנות מהרשימה" or on an <Activity> re-show; "הזמנה מרשימה נוספת" starts afresh. (7, 20) A number in free text stays in the name only as one or two digits right after the name's words, before the mobile ("דנה כהן 2", as pinned); any other goes to `dropped` ("לא נקלט: 45"): a wage after the mobile ("45", "38.5", "8,500", "₪7.200"), a year ("1990"), an age in brackets ("(17)"), an ID's check digit ("12345678/2"), and a number in a cell of its own on a line typed by hand ("יוסי לוי, 24, 052-…", a single comma or pipe line too: free text now joins a line's cells with a tab). `NUMBER_LABEL` adds "מס' עובד", "שכר", "שכר לשעה", "wage" and "salary", and a passport's label leaves with its letters ("דרכון PA1234567"); a currency sign or a slash left at a name's edge goes. With no header, a second column of single words joins the name only when most of its cells hold no digits ("45ש״ח" never does). A single typed line is still free text (the finding's other way), its wage gone all the same. (8) A line's removal toast, and its "ביטול", go as an import is made, taken back, or "הזמנה מרשימה נוספת" starts: pressed behind the done card it brought the old list back and left the focus on the page. (9) "ביטול" after a removal gives the focus back to what removed the lines once their rows are drawn again, in view: the line's "הסרת השורה" (`data-remove-line`) or "הסרת # השורות בלי מספר נייד" (`data-remove-no-phone`); its time up with the focus on it, the line now in its place, else the preview's title, or the done card's. Never the field, off screen on a phone, its keyboard opening over the lines. (10) A dash of any kind beside a digit (U+2010–U+2015 and U+2212: WhatsApp's U+2011 inside direction marks, Word's en dash) reads as "-" in `rowsOf`; a name's own dash stays, and `pasted-text`'s `clean`, so the menu's reader, is unchanged. (11, 19) In a tab paste, a row of one filled cell that holds a mobile (a title's "איש קשר: 050-…") no longer ends the header search: the header under it is found, and the title is a title, not a person to invite. (13) "N הזמנות כבר קיבלו קישור, ולכן נשארו" is said politely as it appears. (14) The refusal is drawn after the lines, beside the status line above the press, and brought into view as it arrives. (15) Tablet and computer: the stuck column of the role and the paste is capped to the screen and scrolls on its own (as the chat's and the orders' columns), so the field "תיקון השורה" focuses comes into view. (16) Not done: each removal's words said twice (the toast's live region, then the focused "ביטול"'s description) is `@ui/status-toast`'s, which this PR doesn't change, and every screen with `focusAction` has it (12); its fix (the region quiet and the words in the button's name, as `UndoRow`) is a change of its own to the component, its tests and /design. (18) A typed list's second column of mobiles, filled on some lines and beside the first one's on a line, is a phone column all the same: `phoneColumns`, as with tabs, so a parent's number is never read as the person's on a line whose own cell is empty. (21) `peopleImportRows` refuses a row with anything more (`text`, `dropped`, `line`). (22) The twin takes `&fail=import` and `&fail=undo`, whose stand-ins refuse. Defaults, for the decisions page: (9) a number kept in a free-text name is one or two digits right after it, before the mobile; (10) a run of digits that starts with another country's code is one number, so a line with "+44 20 … 050-…" and no separator between them is `invalidPhone` until fixed; (11) a tab paste's line of one cell with a mobile above a header row is a title. Tests: unit `people-import.test.ts` (109, from 100: foreign numbers in a phone column, without a header and in free text, the dropped zero from a first group only, 00 972 and +972 still read; five dashes and U+2011 in marks and contacts' pairs, a name's en dash kept; a title with a contact's mobile above a header, a row of cells with one still ending the search; six pairs of name headers before and after the person's, a father's beside first and last names, "שם תפקיד" as the role's column; a column of sums never joined; a sparse second column of mobiles; eleven numbers out of free-text names, single comma and pipe lines, a typed line read as free text; four WhatsApp prefixes and a sender's number; names equal to named roles and those roles read when parted; `peopleImportRows` with three extra keys). Each new reader test failed against the reader as it was (9 of 9); the rows' extra keys pass on both, a guard. The twin's pastes read as before (checked: the same lines for whatsapp, sheet and problems). e2e `design.spec.ts`: "ביטול" after the headings and after line 5 gives the focus back to their buttons, line 5's in view; the WhatsApp test records every request and none carries a number of the list; new, the counts said once and not again after taking back, and a removal's "ביטול" gone with the import; new, `&fail=import` (the refusal after the lines and in view, the press focused, a new alert for the same refusal) and `&fail=undo` (inviting again while "ביטול" is up brings the done card back with `errors.removeFailed`, the focus on "מחיקת ההזמנות", and no second import). Not tested: (5) and the <Activity> half of (6), (13) (the twin keeps no invite) and (15). Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check` (5228 tests). - After the third check: 10 findings, all confirmed, three of them the same from two lenses, so 7 fixes, all made. (1, 7) `NUMBER_LABEL` takes "עובד" only after "מס'" or "מספר" ("מס' עובד 12"), and "שכר" only with a colon or "לשעה". So a bare "עובד" before the number is the line's role again: "דנה כהן עובד 050-…" is a worker from the line, never the managers a list was set to. And "דוד שכר 050-…" keeps his surname. A wage written "שכר 45" after the mobile now leaves "שכר" in the name; the number still goes. (2, 10) A run is no longer foreign when "+" comes before a 0 ("+054-…", "+0527654321", in free text and in a phone column), since no country's code starts with 0. Nor when "00" and at most one digit (1, 7) come before a group with 0 first ("001 050-…", "מס' עובד 001 050-…", "דנה כהן, 001, 050-…"): the mobile is read and "001" goes to `dropped`. "0038 050 123 45 67", "00 38 050 …" and "00380 (0)50 …" stay foreign, now pinned, since Ukraine's 050 is written as Israel's. So the finding's wider rule (any group with 0 first) was not taken; see default (12). (3, 8) Only a named role of one word that no built-in role is called (`nameLike`: "בר", "טבח", "מארחת", "Host") keeps the two-words rule, and is read at a line's start only as a field of its own. A built-in role's word or the business's word for one ("ניהול", "מנהלת", "בעלים", "עובדת", "צוות") and a role of two words ("אחראי משמרת") are read at either end again, after a one-word name too: "דנה ניהול 050-…", "ניהול דנה כהן 050-…", "דנה בעלים 050-…" (one by one), "דנה עובדת 050-…" (a worker on a managers' list). (4, 9) First and last names come before a name's header in other words only when both columns are there ("Nickname"). Beside a first name alone, the full name's column is read ("שם העובד המלא", "Employee full name") and the first name's column is left out. "שם המשפחה" and "השם הפרטי" are the last and first names' headers. (5) A typed list's second column of mobiles blocks (`phoneColumns`) only when it sits beside the first on a line and also holds the number on a line with no mobile of its own in the first. When every line's own mobile is in place, it is left out and named in `ignored`, as before the second review. (6) "הסרת השורה" and "הסרת # השורות בלי מספר נייד" take `ignoreHeldEnter`: Enter held on "ביטול" brings the lines back and doesn't remove them again from the button the focus returned to. (9) "תפקיד" and "role" are out of `NOT_THE_PERSON`. A role's name ("שם תפקיד", "שם התפקיד", "Role name") is read as the role's column by `ROLES_NAME`, tested on the header with its marks. So a name and a role together ("שם / תפקיד", "Name / Role", "Name (Role)", "שם + תפקיד") is the name's column again, and its lines are not all `noName`. Default, for the decisions page: (12) "00" and a code of two digits or more before a group with 0 first ("0038 050 …") is a foreign number. So an employee's number of four or five digits with zeros first, right before the mobile with no separator ("0012 050-…"), is `invalidPhone` until fixed. Tests: unit `people-import.test.ts` (112, from 109). New: an employee's number with zeros first and a stray plus; a full name beside a first name alone, in both orders, with "Nickname" beside first and last, "שם המשפחה" and "השם הפרטי"; seven roles read off a one-word name or at a line's start, two owners, and four worker words on a managers' list. Added to tests already there: three more foreign numbers; "Role name" and four headers of a name and a role; a typed line with two mobiles above four; "דוד שכר"; "שכר:" and "שכר לשעה"; "ברמן דנה כהן" and "דנה מנהלת" beside named roles. Seven of the nine tests new or changed failed against the reader at 43a4e600; the foreign numbers' and the wage's pass on both, as guards. e2e `design.spec.ts`: Enter held on line 5's "ביטול" takes the line back, and the line stays with its "הסרת השורה" focused. Checked here: `pnpm lint:fix`, `pnpm check` (5231 tests). Not run here: `pnpm build`, Playwright (CI is the first run of the new e2e step).
- Run here again after the second review and the third check, against a local `pnpm build`: the import's 60 tests in `design.spec.ts` at 390, 820 and 1440. Before this commit one failed at every width, on a real bug of the second review's: the counts were said only when they changed after the first edit, and the first edit was known by a ref the effect never ran for, so typing that left the counts as they were (a space after the twin's list) said nothing at all. The edits are now a count in state the effect reads: each pause after typing says the counts unless the same were said already, and starting a new list counts from 0 again. All 60 pass.

## 2026-11-26 · chat · inviting from a list (הזמנה מרשימה), part 2: who hasn't got a link yet, on /team

- On chat/pending-links from origin/main 0578e09e (#265). The plan's PR 2 of section 7 ("מסך הצוות: מי שעוד לא קיבל קישור", its 1.6, 3.1 and 3.6), under Orel's decisions of 25.11.2026: each person's link is made at the moment of sending ("יצירת קישור", then WhatsApp or a copy), /team keeps "עוד לא קיבלו קישור", and the app sends nothing to anyone. Built beside PR 1 (chat/import-reader: the reader, the words of the paste and the import twin), which isn't merged; nothing here needs it. One migration, no new npm package, no new `packages/ui` component (`SendLinkRow` is made of existing ones and lives in app/, as the plan says), so no /design gallery entry. **Left for PR 5, as asked: the import twin's send-list state** (`/design/shell/team/import?state=send`), which draws PR 1's `PeopleImportView`; the /team twin's pending rows are here.
- **Migration A**, `20261128000200_invite_links_pending.sql` (no branch on origin or here uses the number; main's newest was 20261128000100): `invites.link_pending boolean not null default false`, with its comment. `private.mark_links_pending()` (sql, security definer, `search_path ''`, revoked from public, anon and authenticated) marks once every unclaimed invite that no `invite.create` or `invite.send` row of its own business names (`details ->> 'invite'`), and the migration runs it; on a local database the seed comes after, so nothing is marked there. The trigger `invites_link_given` (`before update of token_hash`, `private.invite_link_given()`, plpgsql, `search_path ''`, revoked the same) clears the flag when an open row gets a new hash, so `team_invite_link`, `team_invite`'s refresh, `issue_invite_link` and `admin_invite`'s renewal clear it and none of the four is redefined; `team_invite_cancel` closes the row in the same update and leaves it; a write of the same hash leaves it; nothing sets it back. `public.create_business` is 20261020000100's copied whole (`create or replace`, the same signature, so its grants stay service_role only), with its invites saved `link_pending` true and a comment. New, `public.team_invite_first_link(actor, invite, new_token_hash)` (plpgsql, security definer, `search_path ''`, revoked from public, anon and authenticated, granted to service_role only): it locks the open invite `for update`; while `link_pending` holds it returns `public.team_invite_link(...)`, unchanged, with every check it makes; once not, it refuses 42501 to anyone who doesn't edit that invite's team (`private.holds_perm(…, 'team.edit')`, which asks for an active membership) and P0002 with hint `linked` to anyone who does; an invite no longer open is left to `team_invite_link` (P0002, no hint); of two presses at once the second waits for the lock and is refused. `team_invite_link` isn't redefined, and "קישור חדש" still calls it, as it replaces a link on purpose. `public.admin_business` (20261117000700's copied whole; checked with diff) gives each person `link_pending` (false for a member) and an invite with it the status `invited` whatever its date, listed with the open ones: its statuses stay the three the code before reads. `public.admin_businesses` (20261107000900's; only its two counts differ) counts such an invite among the open ones, never the expired. Both `create or replace` with the same signatures, their grants given again. `$$` counted with `grep -o '\$\$' | wc -l`: 12 in the migration (six bodies), 44 in the pgTAP, each even. RLS unchanged: the invites' one select policy and the table grant cover the column. `packages/db/database.types.ts` by hand, as the generator writes it: `link_pending: boolean` in Row, `link_pending?: boolean` in Insert and Update, between `invited_by` and `named_role`, and the function `team_invite_first_link` between `team_invite_cancel` and `team_invite_link`; no existing function's signature changed.
- **@domain.** `TeamInvite.linkPending?`; `InviteState` gains `pending` (`inviteState`: pending whatever its date, so the wizard's invites never read "פג תוקף"); `buildTeam` puts them first, by name, unnamed last, then id (`byNameThenId`), and `counts.pending`, with `open` and `expired` counting only invites with a link (the subtitle's "# הזמנות ממתינות" and the expired hint say what they said). `TeamProgress.pending`: `teamProgress` counts every invite waiting for a link, whatever its role (a second owner's and an accountant's too), as `counts.pending` does, while `joined`, `invited` and `expired` stay on the staff, so when the step is done is unchanged. `NO_TEAM_PROGRESS`; `teamStepWords` (pure): while any wait for a link it says so, after who joined once one of the staff did (`donePending`, plan 3.1), else alone (`pending`); with none waiting, `done` or `doneWaiting` once one joined, else `waiting`, `expired`, `none`. `firstStepHref` leads to /team while any wait. `whatsappToHref(phone, text)` in share.ts: `https://wa.me/<digits of the E.164>?text=…`, throwing on anything not E.164; the text names nobody.
- **@db.** `openInvites` reads `link_pending` first, then (missing column) the named-role read, then the oldest, each `readAllPages`; `getTeam` gives `linkPending` (false before the migration). `issueFirstInviteLink` in @db/invites calls `team_invite_first_link` and reads `linked` before `teamInviteError`. `getBusinessSetup` in @db/admin parses the three statuses with its Zod enum, as before, and `link_pending` (default false, so a database before the migration reads as before), and gives `SetupPerson.status` `pending` for an invited one with it.
